Everything past M10, the CHERI-CompCert **secure-compilation criterion** (robust preservation; the *functional* backend already landed in M1), the **R-05-023a validation instrument** (the decompilation-into-logic producer of the §5 post-CompCert records, whose first non-fast-path artifact also opens the checker-binary question booked in [architectural-alternatives.md](../background/architectural-alternatives.md)), the binary-level constant-time verifier, the masking obligations (the *d*-probing and composition theorems on the crypto artifacts with the Coco-class netlist discharge, R-05-004a, R-15-053a, §4), the R-16-008f detection theorem, the certifying-Rust *certificate* mode, the full VST refinement proofs, WCET, and RTL ⊑ Sail refinement, is the hardening program of §5/§6/§18, each piece replacing a golden-model component *in place* and checked against the reference this plan produces.
Three statements are owed at that program's opening rather than inside it. The **five fallback positions**: the RTL ⊑ Sail arrow is the least-built layer of the stack (R-17-039) and the hedge deletions spend it (R-17-037), so what each of the PMP-backstop, IOMMU, MTE, shadow-stack and initialization-plane rejections becomes under a late or partial close is stated per rejection and read per block (R-17-039a). What the opening owes there is the *instantiation* rather than the writing: which blocks landed on which rung, what the FEV evidence covers on each, and which imported cores still stand at evidence tier, partial coverage being what the three-route ladder produces by construction. The **reduction theorem's verification plan**, the combined-adversary route being already selected (R-17-058d), so the opening states a plan beside the masking obligations rather than a choice. And the **R-15-053a bring-up characterization**, rehearsed on the FPGA and executed at first silicon, the rehearsal validating the harness and never the axiom, an FPGA's leakage being the wrong silicon.

**Hardening opens through reviewed semantic interfaces, not by assigning all proofs serially.** The cells below price the opening plan and the named Coq objects, not the whole hardening program. Prepare source and licence readings within each task; the probing model owns the circuit, leakage and adversary interfaces. Arithmetic and Boolean composition can be developed in parallel after those interfaces and their predicates land. The combined reduction may prepare its statement independently but closes only on the actual detection, countermeasure and composition premises. Unpriced prerequisites get owners and estimates before implementation, not an assumed proof status.

Each task starts with a concrete witness and a refuted alternative, then extends to its full quantified statement. Reuse the admitted prover environment and upstream statements; do not reproduce a foreign toolchain unless that experiment decides a stated uncertainty. Integration audits the exact assumption sets and shared model revision.

Its translation to a conditional-probability bound is informal, independent replication is unestablished, and neither the required arbitrary-order leakage model nor R-17-058d follows from it. This is the crown jewel whose wrongness no theorem beneath it catches, so the item is priced as specification authoring under R-05-150 review and not as proof engineering.

  * **Check:** prove the cardinality-to-probability connection wherever that source is used, then the required *d*-order composition, fresh-mask renewal and pipeline claims in the accepted glitch/transition model, with the algebraic-to-hardware arithmetic relation stated at its actual consumer. An independently replayed first-order cardinality theorem alone cannot close this item. A failed source match changes the proof approach and leaves the required local theorem open.

It is an engineering allowance for authored proof work, not a measured port or a reduction earned by finding a Lean artifact. Review the arbitrary-order statement before splitting independent AES and Keccak instantiations; reuse admitted gadget lemmas, with no assumption that the arithmetic lane proves the Boolean laws.
  * PINI at arbitrary order over GF(2) on the NI and SNI ladder beneath it, glitch-robust, for the HPC and DOM gadget families the datapath carries, instantiated on the AES S-box and the Keccak χ the R-05-004a secret path runs through.

EasyCrypt is its own logic and its own checker rather than a Coq library, its masking line is NI and SNI over GF(2^n) and predates PINI, and the 2026 movement in the area is in checkers rather than in provers. One 2026 result is pinned for its technique and not its artifact, probing security routed through conditional independence in a separation logic for exactly that, and it is stated on paper with no mechanization.

Shared field lemmas may help, but they supply no proof of the required HPC/DOM gadgets under the selected leakage model. No reduction is credited without a matching local theorem.

Missing detection or countermeasure artifacts return to their separately priced owners. Close only against their checked constants and the shared model revision, without weakening the fault quantifier to fit an available proof.
  * The selected route's combined claim, machine-checked on the artifact: every fault the single-fault-per-activation hypothesis admits on the masked datapath's activation is either effective, landing in the R-16-008f detection statement, or confined to one share's computation, landing inside the R-15-053a probing bound at unreduced order.
  * **No compatible combined reduction is qualified by the survey.** VERICA/FIVER and related tools provide external checker evidence; CAPA, M&M and CINI-class constructions have their own models and are not the selected countermeasure. The Coq theorem shape at CompCert's RTL level, recorded in [the fault lineage](../background/inspirations.md#the-masking-and-fault-countermeasure-lineage-dom-to-matchi-the-netlist-level-discharge-and-the-theorem-shapes-the-two-axioms-import), informs the detection side and supplies no complete proof of the combined case.
  * **The range prices the reduction over accepted premises.** Its case split still needs the actual R-16-008f detection theorem and countermeasure construction, whose full implementations are not costed by this cell. Q22g assigns those missing slices; a source-level template or a conditional theorem cannot be counted as their implementation.

## Estimate and schedule basis

* Completed estimates are actual elapsed session intervals with overlapping build waits apportioned approximately.
* Open work classes:
  * A · pinning, configuration, and mechanical curation
  * B · semantic porting and tool re-homing with differential tests
  * C · fresh systems authoring with functional tests
  * D · RTL and FPGA work
* Confidence: every open item's range spans roughly a factor of two about its midpoint, and a class believed softer is priced by widening its own items' ranges rather than by a second figure stated over the total.
* Grand total: the sum of the item cells, 1,592.5 h midpoint over a 1,156–2,029 h range.
* **The authority class is the calibration, and it is measured rather than assumed.** Taking each completed attended item's earliest recorded estimate, which [the calibration record](#calibration-record) carries beside the item's pool, against its recorded actual, across the sixty-one items carrying both, gives 235.9 h actual against 310.4 h estimated, a ratio of 0.76. The error is not uniform and in one pool it runs the other way: forty-six items whose authority is in this repository ran at **0.71**, twelve that read, pin, install or measure an external thing ran at **0.76**, and the three that authored against an external authority ran at **1.42**. Neither overrun is random. M0.8d authored FEC decoders and Keccak against FIPS 202, the ACVP vectors and TS 38.212, and its own finding is that the two defects the known-answer vectors found were both transcriptions; M0.12 authored a corpus, an assembler, a dialect table and an ELF composer from nothing, because no toolchain has one. The deepest under-runs are equally consistent, the two lowest ratios in the record being e3 at 0.13 and e4 at 0.14.
* **The ratio is a prior on range width and not a re-price of midpoints**, which is what the conventions above already require of a class-X cell, and which is what the calibrated figure states separately rather than folding in. Its own weakness is stated: n = 3 in the pool that matters. It is every completed item that authored against an external authority, every one of them overran, all for the same reason, and the consequence is asymmetric, so it is treated as a widening rather than a multiplier. **Turning the prior into a measurement is S19's, and what that item measured is why the pool has not grown**: every completed item that has authored against an external authority since the fit was taken ran under fan-out, so it is on the other clock and outside this pool by the rule below.
* **The agent-parallel series is fitted on its own record and the two fits are not pooled**, which is S19's ruling. Taking each completed agent-parallel item's earliest recorded estimate, which [the agent-parallel record](#the-agent-parallel-series) carries beside its pool, against its recorded actual, across the twenty-eight items carrying both, gives 112.8 h actual against 289.5 h estimated, a ratio of 0.39: sixteen items whose authority is in this repository ran at **0.37**, four that read an external thing ran at **0.61**, and the eight that authored against an external authority ran at **0.33**. **The pool the class-X convention rests on falls on opposite sides of one in the two modes**, 1.42 attended against 0.33 here, so pooling would not widen the class-X prior but delete it, and it would delete it on a conversion between the two clocks that no item has been measured on both of. This ratio prices no cell, which mode an item lands in not being known when the item is priced.
* **The strategy the classes imply is one sentence: move work across the authority line, and generate what is currently authored.** What has not moved is the class-X residue before the M8a gate, which is sixteen open cells, six of them M1.2's children, and which the gate figures above put at more than half that gate's hours.
* Horizon: at 10–20 attended hours per week, the 1,115.5 h remaining is 56–112 attended weeks and the critical chain's 113.5 h midpoint 6–11 of them, both arithmetic `tools/check.py` recomputes over the cells and the stated rate, and neither a promise.
* At that rate the M8a gate is set by the critical chain rather than by the bill, and the chain is lane 1's to release. Review capacity was the constraint beyond three lanes; the two-tier landing rule and the unattended runner are what move it, so lane count is bounded by the register acts that gate a lane rather than by attention at the end of one.

### Calibration record

One row per completed attended item, in document order: the pool its authority fell in, `I` for authority inside this repository, `X-read` for an item that read, pinned, installed or measured an external thing, and `X-authored` for one that authored against an external authority, beside the earliest estimate its cell recorded, read from the plan's own history at the item's first pricing. The actual stays in the item's cell and is read from there rather than restated here; an item that never carried an estimate reads `n/a` in both columns and is outside the fit, and an agent-parallel item carries no row here, its actual being on the other clock and its row being in [the record below](#the-agent-parallel-series). `tools/check.py` holds the record total over the completed attended items in both directions and derives every ratio and count the calibration states from it.

#### The agent-parallel series

One row per completed item marked `agent-parallel`, in document order, on the same convention as the record above: the pool its authority fell in, and the earliest estimate its cell recorded, with the actual read from the item's own cell rather than restated. **This is a second record rather than a fourth column**, because the two fits are never pooled and because the row shape `tools/check.py` reads the record above by is three cells wide. `n/a` in both columns is an item outside the fit, which here has two grounds rather than one: an item that never carried an estimate, S19, S20, S21, S22, M0.20, M3.4d and I15 being the seven, and an item whose estimate and actual were taken over different scopes, M3.4a carrying the F-205l residue it shed at exit, and Q2c/Q18/Q21a/Q21b measuring closing review or validation over existing designs or implementations rather than their original full scope. Those closing passes use conservative provision-to-handoff interval bounds where dispatch clocks were not captured; they supply no matched estimate/actual pair. `tools/check.py` holds this record total over the completed agent-parallel items in both directions and derives every ratio and count stated over it.
